bc,bw,bs,bf=buffer.create,buffer.writeu8,buffer.tostring,buffer.fill local work local ok,result=pc(function() if type(ls)~="function" then return end local s=t.p;t.p=nil if type(s)~="string" or #s%5==1 then return end local groups=#s//5;local tail=#s%5 work=bc(groups*4+(tail>0 and tail-1 or 0)) local at=1;local offset=0 while at<=#s do local count=math.min(5,#s-at+1);local n=0 for j=0,4 do local v=84 if j<count then local c=by(s,at+j);if c<33 or c>117 then return end;v=c-33 end n=n*85+v end if n>4294967295 then return end local take=count==5 and 4 or count-1 for j=0,take-1 do bw(work,offset,n//2^((3-j)*8)%256);offset+=1 end at+=count end s=nil local text=bs(work) bf(work,0,0);work=nil local fn=ls(text) text=nil if type(fn)~="function" then return end if type(sf)=="function" and type(env)=="table" then sf(fn,env) end return fn(table.unpack(ag,1,ag.n)) end) if work then bf(work,0,0);work=nil end t.p=nil for j=1,ag.n do ag[j]=nil end ag.n=0 if ok then return result end end})(...)
